Let's outline implementation:
- Functions top-level, no closures/lambdas.
- `make_fft(a, n)`? For objective, use rfft with size power of two >=2n-1.
```
def conv_full_finite(a):
    n=a.size
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For objective and gradient, repeated rfft. To speed, precompute size maybe. But many lengths. Use `scipy.fft.next_fast_len` if available (numpy maybe). We can implement power2. `np.fft.rfftn`? 1D.
- Score: `2*n*np.max(c)/float(np.sum(a)**2)`; since sum fixed maybe 1. To match evaluator if values >1000? We'll scale sum=1 so no clip. But if optimizer yields values >1000 no.
- Smooth objective: Use `soft_max(c, beta)` maybe `m + logsumexp(c-m)/beta`. But max coefficient scale ~1/n; beta maybe n * 10? If using p=norm also sum scale. Alternatively Lp: `Cp = np.sum(c**p)`. Since c positive and sum=1, Cp ~ (2n)*(1/n)^p. For p=8, maybe under/overflow small. Use log-sum-exp for p? Could use `np.sum(c**p)` safe if c~0.001, p 8 ~1e-24 okay. For n small c~0.01 p16=1e-32. Implement direct shifts efficiently? We can exploit if peaks sorted? Let's write loop:
```
def gradient_top_shifts(a, peaks):
    n=a.size
    D=np.zeros(n)
    for k in peaks:
        lo=max(0, k-n+1)
        hi=min(n-1, k)
        if lo>hi: continue
        # D[lo:hi+1] += a[k-lo : k-hi+1] (reverse)
        D[lo:hi+1] += a[k-lo : k-hi+1][::-1]
    D *= 2.0/len(peaks)
    return D
```
Check slice: for lo..hi, j=k-i ranges k-lo down to k-hi. `a[k-lo : k-hi+1]` length hi-lo+1, reversed gives [k-lo, ..., k-hi]? Actually original [k-lo, k-lo-1, ..., k-hi]? We want for i=lo: j=k-lo; for i=hi: j=k-hi. So reverse of a[k-hi : k-lo+1] perhaps. Let's test n=3,k=2 lo=0 hi=2? maximum i=2. Want D[0]=a2, D[1]=a1, D[2]=a0. `a[k-lo : k-hi+1]` = a[2:0]? empty. Now local search using multiplicative weights:
```
def mwu_local(a, n, deadline, rng, initial_lr, k_schedule, momentum):
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score...
    lr=0.1? 
    v=np.zeros(n)
    not_improve=0
    iter=0
    while time<deadline:
       c=conv; maxc; score=...
       if score<best_score: update best
       peaks = top k indices (argpartition). K maybe variable.
       D=grad_top_shifts(a, peaks)
       # Optional add small positive floor to avoid underflow: D_min = min(D)*? maybe
       v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
       # Scale step: lr * (v / (mean(v)+eps)) maybe to make average decay ~lr
       step = lr * v / (v.mean()+eps)
       a = a * np.exp(-step)
       a = normalize(a)
       if not finite or sum==0: reset from best, lr*=0.5
       # Adaptive: track score over window; if no improve, reduce lr or increase K/noise.
    return best,best_score
```

Algorithm multiplicative with gradient of top/Lp:
```
def mwu_step(a, D, momentum=0.5, max_step=0.5):
    v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
    dptp = v.max()-v.min()
    if dptp < 1e-12: return a
    step = max_step * (v - v.min()) / dptp
    a = a * np.exp(-step)
    a = normalize(a)
```
This reduces variables with larger D by up to max_step, leaves min unchanged. `max_step` maybe 0.5 (e^(-0.5)=0.607). Good. Use momentum to smooth. This is like normalized multiplicative weight.
- Maybe also inverse for variables with zero D? They become relatively larger; good.

Need D for Lp dense. Compute c, w = c^{p-1} (maybe scaled). D via Hankel FFT. For p=2: w=c. For p=4: w=c^3. To avoid overflow, cap w by max. Since c ~1/n, powers tiny; scaling irrelevant. Use `w = np.power(c + eps, p-1)`; scaling constant irrelevant; but if underflow to zero for tail, okay. To compute D, we can use reverse convolution with w. But if w scaled tiny, D tiny; standardization handles. Need w float64; if all zero due underflow (n large p large), choose smaller p or scale w by max. Do `w = w / (np.max(w)+eps)` to avoid underflow/overflow. Then D normalized.

Hankel FFT details:
```
def hankel_product(w, a, n):
    L=2*n-1
    revw = w[::-1]
    N=1
    while N < L + n -1: N*=2
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(revw, N)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa, N)
    # need indices q=2n-2-i
    start = 2*n-2
    return 2.0 * conv[start- n +1 : start+1][::-1]? Let's derive.
```
We need g[i] = 2 * conv[2n-2 - i] for i=0..n-1. That is indices from 2n-2 down to n-1. So slice `conv[n-1:2*n-1]` is ascending n-1..2n-2. Reverse gives descending. `g = 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]`. Check n=3: indices 2..4, conv[2,3,4] reverse [4,3,2] => g0=conv4,g1=conv3,g2=conv2 correct. Good.

def local_search(a, n, deadline, rng, p=4.0, max_step=0.4, momentum=0.5, noise_interval=500, noise_scale=0.1):
    a=normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score...
    v=np.zeros(n); prev_c? 
    steps=0; last_improve=0
    p_current=p
    while time<deadline:
       c=conv_full(a)
       maxc=max(c); score=...
       if score<best_score: update best; last_improve=steps
       if steps-last_improve > threshold: adjust p or max_step or reset.
       w = c**(p-1); w/=max
       D=hankel_gradient(w,a,n)
       # add tiny positive floor to D? No, relative.
       v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
       # standardize v: subtract min; if ptp too small break
       ptp = v.max()-v.min()
       if ptp < 1e-12: # do random perturb
          a = perturb(a, rng)
          continue
       step = max_step * (v-v.min())/ptp
       a_new = a * np.exp(-step)
       a_new = normalize(a_new)
       # check score maybe not each step? Need line search? To save, compute every step; FFT already for c? We already c. Updating requires new c next loop.
       a = a_new
       steps++
       if steps%noise_interval==0:
           a = perturb(a,rng, noise_scale*(1)) # maybe accept only if better? We'll just do; best kept.
    return best,best_score
```

Pseudo:
```
def local_search_maxfixed(a, n, deadline, rng, p=4.0, max_step=0.25, momentum=0.6, check_every=1):
    a = clip; 
    # scale to max1
    c=conv_full(a)
    maxc=...
    if maxc<=0: fallback
    a = a / sqrt(maxc)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score...
    current_score=best_score
    v=np.zeros(n)
    steps=0; no_improve=0; reject_streak=0
    max_step_cur=max_step
    while time<deadline:
       if no_improve > 1000:
           # diversify: reset to best, apply random perturb, reset v, reduce/increase p
       # compute D from current c? we need current c. We have c from previous? At start compute. After update, we compute new c and scale; keep c relative? After scaling, c_scaled = c_new / maxc (max1). We can use that c_scaled for weights. Need sum for score.
       c = c / maxc? If we scaled a, c values scale by 1/maxc. Keep `c` updated.
       maxc = max(c) should ~1.
       score=2*n*maxc/(sum(a)**2)
       update best
       if score > current_best? no_improve++ etc.
       # weights
       w = np.power(c, p-1)
       w = w / (w.max()+eps)
       D = hankel_gradient(w,a,n)
       # maybe add small noise to D to break symmetry: D += rng.normal(0, D.std()*0.01)
       v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
       ptp = v.max()-v.min()
       if ptp < 1e-12:
           # random step
           a = perturb ...
           continue
       step = max_step_cur * (v - v.min())/ptp
       old_a=a.copy(); old_score=score
       a = a * np.exp(-step)
       # Maybe clip tiny; ensure positive
       a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
       cnew = conv_full(a)
       maxnew = cnew.max()
       if maxnew <=0 or not finite: reject
       a = a / np.sqrt(maxnew)
       # after scaling, c scaled = cnew / maxnew; score
       new_score = 2*n*maxnew/(np.sum(a)**2)  # but maxnew before scaling; after scaling max=1, sum scaled, score=2n/sum_scaled². sum_scaled=sum/sqrt(maxnew). Equivalent 2n*maxnew/sum².
       if finite and (new_score < score - 1e-12 or rng.random()<0.02): accept
           a, score, c = a, new_score, cnew/maxnew
           # if improved best...
           no_improve=0; reject_streak=0
           # adaptive: if new_score < best_score*0.999? increase max_step? 
       else:
           # reject; restore, reduce max_step; maybe try smaller step from old
           a=old_a; score=old_score; c=c/m? current
           max_step_cur *=0.8; reject_streak+=1
           if reject_streak>20: reset to best with perturb, max_step=0.2, v=0
       steps++
```

`ceil_power2(m)`:
```
def ceil_power2(m):
    return 1 if m <=1 else 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
Actually bit_length of m-1.

`convolution_fft(a, size=None)`:
```
def convolution_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    size=ceil_power2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    if c.size > 2*n-1: c=c[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0,c) # in-place
    return c
```
Use `out`? OK.

`score_from_c(c,n)`: `return float(2*n*np.max(c) / (np.sum(a)**2))` but if c already max1 and a outside? We'll make `score_from_a_c(a,c)`.

In local loop, after scaling max1, a values may have sum large ~√n. For n=8192 sum~96, okay.

`make_noise(n, rng, nfreq, center=False)`:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq, phase=None):
   size=ceil_power2(n)
   spec=(rng.normal(0,1,size//2+1)+1j*rng.normal(0,1,size//2+1))
   # keep low freq symmetric
   m=max(1,nfreq)
   # set high to zero, maybe taper
   spec[m:] *= np.linspace(0,0.3,m? )? simpler zero
   spec[m:]=0
   x=np.fft.irfft(spec, size)[:n]
   x -= x.mean()
   s=x.std()
   if s>1e-12: x/=s
   return x
```
This creates smooth noise. For shape perturb, use multiple calls combined.

Local dense gradient implementation:
```
def dense_gradient(w, a, n):
    L=2*n-1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != L:
       # pad/crop
    rev = w[::-1].copy()
    N=ceil_power2(L + n - 1)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(rev, N)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa, N)
    s = 2*n - 1
    # indices s - i for i=0..n-1 => conv[n-1:s][::-1]
    g = conv[n-1:s][::-1]  # s=2n-1 exclusive? If indices n-1..2n-2 inclusive => slice n-1:2n-1, yes.
    return 2.0*g
```
Check: L=2n-1, L+n-1=3n-2. s=2n-2 inclusive, slice n-1:2n-1. Good.
Potential numerical tiny negative; gradient can be negative due FFT; but weights positive, product positive; irfft may have tiny neg. Could `g[np.abs(g)<1e-15]=0`? No need.
`w` scale: normalize to max1 to avoid large. `w /= w.max()`; if max=0 return zeros.

`lstep(a,c,n,p, max_step, momentum, v, rng)`:
```
w = c.copy()
if p !=2:
   # c max around1, use power
   w = np.power(c, p-1.0)
maxw = w.max()
if maxw <=0: return None
w /= maxw
D = dense_gradient(w,a,n)
# if D has NaN, fallback
if not finite: return None
# Add tiny noise to D to avoid exact symmetry
if rng.random()<0.3:
   scale = max(D.std(),1e-12)
   D = D + rng.normal(0,0.01*scale,n)
v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
lo = np.min(v); hi=np.max(v); ptp=hi-lo
if ptp < 1e-12:
   return None
step = max_step * (v-lo)/ptp
b = a * np.exp(-step)
# remove zeros
b = np.maximum(b, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
return b,v
```
Note: If D=0 for some coordinates, step min=0, those coordinates unchanged. Good. The relative scaling (subtract min) means total mass can decrease/increase? Before scaling, we don't normalize. Then scale max1. This should be okay. Is subtracting min always beneficial? Constant offset in log update changes all a by constant; before max scaling, global scale irrelevant. Yes.
But what if D has negative values due FFT noise? Weights positive; maybe not. If negative, min could be negative, step positive for some? Use `v_nonneg = np.maximum(v,0)`? Since gradient positive; small negative noise. Compute lo=v.min() but if negative, step at min 0, max positive; variables with negative v increased relative. Fine.

def local_search_maxfixed(a, c, n, deadline, rng, p0=4.0, max_step0=0.2, momentum=0.5):
    a = array; c=conv; a,c=scale_to_max1
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_from_a_c(a,c); current_score=best_score
    v=zeros; max_step=max_step0; p=p0
    no_improve=0; reject=0; steps=0; last_p_change=0
    while time<deadline:
       if no_improve > 500:
           # adjust p
           if p < 16: p *=1.5
           else: p=4.0
           no_improve=0
           # maybe reset max_step
       if reject > 10 or no_improve > 1000:
           # reset to best with perturb
           b = a.copy(); c_cur=c.copy(); score_cur=current_score
           b,c_try = perturb ... maybe not evaluate? Use lowstep small
           # To save, set a=best copy, c=bestc copy, score=best, v=0, max_step=0.2, and apply small random accept
           a=best.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); current_score=best_score; v=zeros; reject=0
           if rng.random()<0.5:
               b,c_new = random_perturb(a,best_c,rng,eta=0.1)
               s=score...
               if s<current_score: accept
           no_improve=0
       cmax=np.max(c); if cmax<=0: break
       # weights power
       w = np.power(c, p-1)
       maxw=np.max(w); if maxw<=0: break
       w/=maxw
       D=dense_gradient(w,a,n)
       if not finite: break
       v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
       ptp = v.max()-v.min()
       if ptp < 1e-12:
           # random direction
           b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
       else:
           step = max_step * (v-np.min(v))/ptp
           b = a*np.exp(-step)
       b = np.maximum(b, tiny)
       cb = convolution_fft(b)
       b,cb=scale_to_max1(b,cb)
       snew=score_from_a_c(b,cb)
       if finite:
          if snew < current_score - 1e-9 or rng.random() < 0.03:
             a=b; c=cb; current_score=snew
             if current_score < best_score:
                 best=a.copy(); best_score=current_score; best_c=c.copy(); no_improve=0
             # adaptive increase step if improved and not too high
             if max_step < 0.5: max_step *=1.05
             reject=0
          else:
             # reject; try smaller step maybe? We reject; reduce max_step
             max_step *=0.85
             reject+=1
          # no_improve if current >= best*(1+1e-6)
          if current_score >= best_score*(1+1e-7): no_improve +=1
          else: no_improve=0
       else:
          max_step*=0.5
       steps+=1
       if steps % 1000 ==0: print progress
    return best,best_score
```
Need maintain `best_c` for reset. If accept small randomness may get stuck in basin; best kept. `no_improve` increments if current not better than best. If current wanders equal, okay.
Potential issue: Comparing new_score to current_score; if accept with probability 0.03 even worse, can slowly deteriorate but best kept.

`lowpass_noise` implementation details:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq):
    size = ceil_power2(n)
    nf = size // 2 + 1
    spec = rng.normal(0.0,1.0,nf)+1j*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,nf)
    k = min(nf, max(1, int(nfreq)))
    # zero high, maybe taper for k..2k to reduce discontinuity
    if k < nf:
        spec[k:] = 0.0
    x = np.fft.irfft(spec, size)[:n]
    x -= x.mean()
    s = x.std()
    if s > 1e-14: x /= s
    else: x = rng.normal(0,1,n); x-=x.mean(); s=x.std(); x/=s
    return x
```
If nfreq small (1), only DC; we zero it by mean subtract. Use nfreq at least2.

`evaluate_scaled(a)`:
```
def evaluate_scaled(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    a,c = scale_to_max1(a,c)
    s = np.sum(a)
    if s <= 0: return None
    score = score_from_a_c(a,c)
    return a,c,score
```
If a evaluates inf, return None.

`random_perturb_current(a,c,rng,eta,use_block)`:
```
def random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, eta, use_block=False, nfreq=None):
   b=a.copy()
   if use_block:
      n=b.size; width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//50), max(2,n//5)))
      start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
      b[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, eta))
      if rng.random()<0.2: b = 0.5*(b+b[::-1])
   else:
      if nfreq is None: nfreq=int(rng.integers(2, min(64,n//16)+1))
      noise = lowpass_noise(b.size, rng, nfreq)
      b *= np.exp(eta * noise)
   b = np.maximum(b, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
   return evaluate_scaled(b)
```
But evaluate_scaled recomputes conv. For local updates, we already have c? Could special. Fine for random.

To avoid too many full conv in local, dense gradient already computed c. But update evaluate needs conv anyway.

`local_search_maxfixed` more precise. Need initialize a,c. Use `a,c = scale_to_max1` after evaluate.
Variables: `best_a, best_c, best_score, current_score`. Use `current_c` for max1 coefficients.
Loop:
```
steps=0; no_improve=0; rejects=0; p=4.0; max_step=0.2; momentum=0.55; v=np.zeros(n)
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
   # adaptive p: p increases with no_improve plateaus
   if no_improve > 300 and p < 8: p=8.0
   elif no_improve > 600 and p < 16: p=16.0
   elif no_improve > 1000:
       # reset
       a = best_a.copy(); c=current_c? best_c.copy(); current_score=best_score; v.fill(0); max_step=0.2; p=4.0; no_improve=0; rejects=0
       # small perturb to escape
       res = random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, 0.15, use_block=rng.random()<0.5, nfreq=None)
       if res is not None:
           a2,c2,s2=res; if s2 < best_score*1.02: a,c,current_score=a2,c2,s2
   # compute gradient
   w = np.power(c, p-1.0)
   maxw = np.max(w)
   if maxw <=0: break
   w = w / maxw
   D = dense_gradient(w,a,n)
   if not np.isfinite(D).all(): break
   # small random jitter
   jitter = rng.random()
   if jitter < 0.2:
       std = D.std()
       if std > 0: D += rng.normal(0, 0.05*std, n)
   v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*D
   vmin = v.min(); vmax = v.max(); span = vmax-vmin
   if span <= 1e-14:
       # random multiplicative

def stretch_sequence(a, target):
   # repeat each element target//n times, if target not multiple pad with last or truncate
   n=a.size
   reps=target//n
   if reps<1: # downsample by averaging
   out=np.repeat(a, reps)
   if out.size<target: out=np.concatenate([out,a[-(target-out.size):]])
   return out[:target]
```

Now `stretch_sequence`:
```
def stretch_sequence(a, target):
    n=a.size
    if target==n: return a.copy()
    reps=max(1, target//n)
    if reps*n > target:
       # simpler if reps=1 but target >n, use np.interp? Repeating with reps1 leaves length n, not target.
    # use np.repeat then pad/truncate.
    if reps < 1:
       # downsample by averaging
       idx=np.linspace(0,n-1,target).astype(int)
       return a[idx]
    out=np.repeat(a, reps)
    if out.size < target:
       # pad by repeating last as needed
       pad = (target - out.size + n -1)//n * n
       out = np.concatenate([out, np.tile(a, pad)])
    return out[:target]
```

Detailed functions:

```
def ceil_power2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`convolution_fft`:
```
def convolution_fft(a):
    n=a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    size=ceil_power2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    out=c[:2*n-1].copy()
    np.maximum(out,0.0,out)
    return out
```

`scale_to_max1`:
```
def scale_to_max1(a,c):
    a=np.array(a,dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    c=np.array(c,dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
       # fallback: set to small ones?
       return None
    inv=1.0/maxc
    a*=inv
    c*=inv
    # maxc after scaling should be1; but if c negative none.
    return a,c
```
If maxc inf due huge? We keep values finite. If returns None, fallback.

`evaluate_scaled`:
```
def evaluate_scaled(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size ==0: return None
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(a).all(): return None
    # if all zero add small
    if a.sum() <= 0: a=np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    c=convolution_fft(a)
    sc=scale_to_max1(a,c)
    if sc is None:
       # fallback
       a=np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64); c=convolution_fft(a); sc=scale_to_max1(a,c)
       if sc is None: return None
    a,c=sc
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-14: return None
    m=float(np.max(c))
    score=float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
    if not np.isfinite(score): return None
    return float_a? tuple (a,c,score)
```
Maybe don't pass c in, recompute.

`score_from_a_c`:
```
def score_from_a_c(a,c):
    s=float(np.sum(a)); m=float(np.max(c))
    if s <=1e-14 or not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
```

`make_c_sequence`:
```
def make_c_sequence(n):
    a=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(1,n):
        a[i]=a[i-1]*((2*i-1)/(2*i))
    c=convolution_fft(a)
    return scale_to_max1(a,c)
```
Should return tuple; if None fallback.

`lowpass_noise` as above. Need use `int(nfreq)`.

`random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, eta, use_block)`:
```
def random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, eta, use_block):
    b=a.copy()
    n=b.size
    if use_block:
       minw=max(1,n//500); maxw=max(minw+1,n//4)
       w=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw))
       st=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-w)))
       corr=rng.normal(0.0, eta)
       b[st:st+w] *= float(np.exp(corr))
       if rng.random() < 0.25:
           b = 0.5*(b + b[::-1])
    else:
       nfreq=int(rng.integers(2, min(64, max(3,n//32))+1))
       noise=lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq)
       b *= np.exp(eta * noise)
       if rng.random() < 0.25:
           b = 0.5*(b + b[::-1])
    b=np.maximum(b, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    return evaluate_scaled(b)
```
Symmetrizing can hurt but maybe okay.

`dense_gradient`:
```
def dense_gradient(w,a,n):
    L=2*n-1
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != L:
       tmp=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
       m=min(w.size,L); tmp[:m]=w[:m]; w=tmp
    maxw=float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(maxw) or maxw <=0:
       return np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    w=w/maxw
    rev=w[::-1].copy()
    N=ceil_power2(L+n-1)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(rev, N)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa, N)
    if conv.size < 2*n:
       # pad? should not
       tmp=np.zeros(2*n,dtype=np.float64); tmp[:conv.size]=conv; conv=tmp
    g=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return 2.0*g
```
Potential memory copy of rev okay.

`local_search_maxfixed`:
Need pass best_a global? It returns best. Use `deadline` maybe slice.
```
def local_search_maxfixed(a, c, n, deadline, rng, p0=4.0, max_step0=0.18, momentum=0.55):
    a,c = scale_to_max1(a,c)  # ensure
    if a is None: return None
    score=score_from_a_c(a,c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
    current_score=score; last_improve=0
    v=np.zeros(n)
    p=p0; max_step=max_step0; rejects=0; steps=0
    tiny=np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed=steps-last_improve
       if elapsed > 250:
           if p < 8.0: p=8.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.18
           elif p < 16.0: p=16.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.15
           elif p < 32.0: p=32.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.12
           else:
               p=p0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.18
       if elapsed > 1200 or rejects > 12:
           a=best_a.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); current_score=best_score; v.fill(0); rejects=0
           res=random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, 0.12, use_block=(rng.random()<0.5))
           if res is not None:
              a2,c2,s2=res
              if s2 < best_score * 1.05:
                  a,c,current_score=a2,c2,s2
                  if s2 < best_score:
                      best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=s2; last_improve=steps
       # weights
       w=np.power(c, p-1.0)
       maxw=float(np.max(w)) if w.size else 0.0
       if not np.isfinite(maxw) or maxw <=0.0:
           break
       w=w/maxw
       D=dense_gradient(w,a,n)
       if not np.isfinite(D).all(): break
       if rng.random() < 0.15:
           std=float(np.std(D))
           if std > 1e-15:
               D = D + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05*std, n)
       v = momentum*v + (1.0-momentum)*D
       vmin=float(np.min(v)); vmax=float(np.max(v)); span=vmax-vmin
       if span <= 1e-14:
           b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n))
       else:
           step = max_step * (v - vmin)/span
           b = a * np.exp(-step)
       b = np.maximum(b, tiny)
       cb = convolution_fft(b)
       scaled = scale_to_max1(b, cb)
       if scaled is None:
           max_step=max(0.02, max_step*0.5); rejects+=1; steps+=1; continue
       b,cb=scaled
       snew=score_from_a_c(b,cb)
       accept=False
       if np.isfinite(snew):
           if snew <= current_score:
               accept=True
           elif snew <= current_score * (1.0 + 0.02):
               accept = rng.random() < 0.15
       if accept:
           a=b; c=cb; current_score=snew
           if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=current_score; last_improve=steps
               print(f"local n={n} new best {best_score:.6f} step={steps} p={p:.1f}")
           max_step = min(0.55, max_step * 1.04)
           rejects=0
       else:
           max_step = max(0.02, max_step * 0.85)
           rejects += 1
           # retry smaller? Could try one half-step if rejects multiple
           if rejects == 4:
               if span > 1e-14:
                   step2 = 0.25*max_step*(v - vmin)/span
                   b2 = a * np.exp(-step2)
                   b2 = np.maximum(b2, tiny)
                   cb2=convolution_fft(b2)
                   sc2=scale_to_max1(b2,cb2)
                   if sc2 is not None:
                       b2,cb2=sc2; s2=score_from_a_c(b2,cb2)
                       if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 <= current_score:
                           a,c=current_score=a? can't assign; need separate
                           a=b2; c=cb2; current_score=s2
                           if current_score < best_score:
                              best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=current_score; last_improve=steps
                           rejects=0
       steps +=1
       if steps % 2000 == 0:
           print(f"local n={n} best={best_score:.6f} cur={current_score:.6f} p={p:.1f} step={max_step:.3f}")
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```
In smaller retry, assignment `a,c=current_score=a?` wrong; fix.
Need if best_score from local is None. Handle.

`search_length`:
```
def search_length(n, rng, deadline, global_best):
    inits=[]
    base=make_c_sequence(n)
    if base is not None:
       a,c=base; s=score_from_a_c(a,c); inits.append((a.copy(),s))
    if global_best is not None:
        ast=stretch_sequence(global_best,n)

```
def search_length(n, rng, deadline, global_best):
    inits=[]
    base=make_c_sequence(n)
    if base is not None:
       a,c=base; s=score_from_a_c(a,c)
       if np.isfinite(s): inits.append((a.copy(), c.copy(), float(s)))
    if global_best is not None:
       gb=np.asarray(global_best,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if gb.size>0:
          st=stretch_sequence(gb,n)
          res=evaluate_scaled(st)
          if res is not None and res[2] < 1.6:
             inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))
    if inits:
       base_a=inits[0][0]
       for _ in range(3):
           res=random_perturb_scaled(base_a, rng, 0.2, use_block=(rng.random()<0.4))
           if res is not None and res[2] < 1.7:
              inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))
       # sym
       for a0,c0,s0 in list(inits):
           b=0.5*(a0+a0[::-1])
           res=evaluate_scaled(b)
           if res is not None and res[2] < s0:
              inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))
    if not inits:
       return None
    inits.sort(key=candidate_key)
    # remove exact duplicates? not needed
    best_a,best_score = inits[0][0], inits[0][2]
    # If best_score already very good, maybe still local? yes.
    # Run local on up to 2 distinct initials
    tries = min(2, len(inits))
    for idx in range(tries):
       rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem <= 30: break
       slice_time = rem / float(tries - idx)
       if slice_time < 30: slice_time = max(10.0, rem/2.0)
       sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + slice_time)
       loc=local_search_maxfixed(inits[idx][0], inits[idx][1], n, sub_deadline, rng)
       if loc is not None and loc[1] < best_score:
           best_a=loc[0]; best_score=loc[1]
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```

def local_search_maxfixed(a, c, n, deadline, rng, p0=4.0, max_step0=0.18, momentum=0.55):
    scaled = scale_to_max1(a,c)
    if scaled is None: return None
    a,c=scaled
    score = score_from_a_c(a,c)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return None
    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
    current=score; last_improve=0
    v=np.zeros(n); p=p0; max_step=max_step0; rejects=0; steps=0
    tiny=np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed=steps-last_improve
        if elapsed > 250:
            if p < 8.0: p=8.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.18
            elif p < 16.0: p=16.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.15
            elif p < 32.0: p=32.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.12
            elif p < 64.0: p=64.0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.10
            else: p=p0; v.fill(0); max_step=0.18
        if elapsed > 1000 or rejects > 10:
            a=best_a.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); current=best_score; v.fill(0); rejects=0
            res=random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, 0.20, (rng.random()<0.5))
            if res is not None and (res[2] < best_score*1.25 or rng.random()<0.35):
                a=res[0].copy(); c=res[1].copy(); current=float(res[2])
                if current < best_score:
                    best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=current; last_improve=steps
        w=np.power(c, p-1.0)
        maxw=float(np.max(w))
        if not np.isfinite(maxw) or maxw <=0: break
        w=w/maxw
        D=dense_gradient(w,a,n)
        if not np.isfinite(D).all(): break
        if rng.random()<0.15:
            std=float(np.std(D))
            if std>1e-15: D = D + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05*std, n)
        v = momentum*v + (1.0-momentum)*D
        vmin=float(np.min(v)); vmax=float(np.max(v)); span=vmax-vmin
        if span <=1e-14:
            b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n))
        else:
            step = max_step * (v - vmin)/span
            b = a * np.exp(-step)
        b=np.maximum(b,tiny)
        cb=convolution_fft(b)
        sc=scale_to_max1(b,cb)
        if sc is None:
            max_step=max(0.02,max_step*0.5); rejects +=1; steps +=1; continue
        b,cb=sc
        snew=score_from_a_c(b,cb)
        accept=False
        if np.isfinite(snew):
            if snew <= current:
                accept=True
            else:
                tol = 0.01 + 0.05/(1.0 + float(elapsed)/500.0)
                if snew <= current*(1.0 + tol) and rng.random()<0.25:
                    accept=True
        if accept:
            a=b; c=cb; current=float(snew)
            if current < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=current; last_improve=steps
                print("local n={} best={:.6f} step={} p={:.1f}".format(n,best_score,steps,p))
            max_step=min(0.55, max_step*1.04)
            rejects=0
        else:
            max_step=max(0.02, max_step*0.85)
            rejects +=1
        steps +=1
        if steps % 3000 ==0:
            print("local n={} best={:.6f} cur={:.6f} p={:.1f} step={:.3f}".format(n,best_score,current,p,max_step))
    return best_a, float(best_score)
```
Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,float(budget_s)-25.0)
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf
    # height_sequence_1 try
    try:
       hs=height_sequence_1
       arr=np.asarray(hs,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size>0:
          arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
          res=evaluate_scaled(arr)
          if res is not None:
             if res[2] < best_score: best_a=res[0]; best_score=float(res[2]); print
    except Exception:
       pass
    lengths=[(1024,120),(2048,500),(4096,1200),(8192,1200)]
    for n, cap in lengths:
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem <= 60: break
       slice_time=min(cap, rem-40)
       if slice_time < 40: break
       sub_deadline=time.monotonic()+slice_time
       res=search_length(n, rng, sub_deadline, best_a)
       if res is not None:
          if res[1] < best_score:
             best_a=res[0]; best_score=float(res[1]); print
    # final polish
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120 and best_a is not None:
       ab=np.asarray(best_a,dtype=np.float64)
       c=convolution_fft(ab)
       loc=local_search_maxfixed(ab,c,len(ab),deadline,rng)
       if loc is not None and loc[1] < best_score:
          best_a=loc[0]; best_score=loc[1]
    # fallback
    if best_a is None:
       base=make_c_sequence(1024)
       if base is None: base=(np.ones(256), convolution_fft(np.ones(256))) ...
       best_a=base[0]
    fin=np.asarray(best_a,dtype=np.float64); fin=np.maximum(fin,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(fin))
    if s <= 1e-12:
       fin=np.ones(1024,dtype=np.float64); s=float(np.sum(fin))
    fin=fin/s
    out=[]
    for x in fin:
       if x > 1e-300: out.append(float(x))
       else: out.append(0.0)
    # verify with evaluator if available
    try:
       actual=evaluate_sequence(out)
       # if actual inf, maybe return sequence un-scaled max1?
       if not np.isfinite(actual):
          out2=[float(max(0.0,x)) for x in np.asarray(best_a)]
          actual2=evaluate_sequence(out2)
          if np.isfinite(actual2): out=out2
    except Exception:
       pass
    return out

```python
"""
Searches for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    J(a) = 2 * n * max(a * a) / (sum(a)^2)

where a * a is ordinary linear convolution.  The code uses:
  * a strong one-sided fractional-powers starting point a_k ~ binom(2k,k)/4^k,
  * max-constant rescaling (so improving means increasing total sum at fixed peak),
  * a deterministic multiplicative-weights local search driven by Lp peaks,
  * random low-frequency / edge / block perturbations for diversification,
  * multiple signal lengths and stretching of the best candidate found so far.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def ceil_power2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def convolution_fft(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = ceil_power2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    out = c[:2 * n - 1].copy()
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out)
    return out


def score_from_a_c(a, c):
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if s <= 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(m) or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    return float(2 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def scale_to_max1(a, c):
    a = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    c = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size == 0 or c.size == 0:
        return None
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return None
    inv = 1.0 / maxc
    a *= inv
    c *= inv
    return a, c


def evaluate_scaled(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return None
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    sc = scale_to_max1(a, c)
    if sc is None:
        return None
    a, c = sc
    score = score_from_a_c(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return None
    return a, c, float(score)


def make_c_sequence(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated, scaled to peak 1."""
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * ((2 * i - 1) / (2 * i))
    c = convolution_fft(a)
    return scale_to_max1(a, c)


def stretch_sequence(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64)
    if int(target) == int(a.size):
        return a.copy()
    if int(target) <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if int(a.size) <= 1:
        return np.full(int(target), float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if int(target) < int(a.size):
        idx = np.linspace(0, a.size - 1, target, dtype=np.float64).astype(np.int64)
        return a[idx]
    reps = max(1, int(target) // int(a.size))
    out = np.repeat(a, reps)
    if out.size < int(target):
        pad_blocks = ((int(target) - out.size) + int(a.size) - 1) // int(a.size)
        pad = np.tile(a, pad_blocks)
        out = np.concatenate((out, pad))
    return out[:int(target)]


def lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq):
    size = ceil_power2(n)
    nf = size // 2 + 1
    spec = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, nf) + 1j * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, nf)
    k = min(nf, max(2, int(nfreq)))
    if k < nf:
        spec[k:] = 0.0
    x = np.fft.irfft(spec, size)[:n]
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    sd = float(np.std(x))
    if sd <= 1e-14:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        x -= float(np.mean(x))
        sd = float(np.std(x))
        if sd <= 1e-14:
            x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            return x
    return x / sd


def random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, eta, use_block):
    b = a.copy()
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    if use_block:
        minw = max(1, n // 500)
        maxw = max(minw + 1, n // 4)
        w = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w)))
        corr = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, eta)))
        b[st:st + w] *= corr
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            b = 0.5 * (b + b[::-1])
    else:
        nfreq = int(rng.integers(2, min(64, max(3, n // 32)) + 1))
        noise = lowpass_noise(n, rng, nfreq)
        b *= np.exp(eta * noise)
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            b = 0.5 * (b + b[::-1])
    b = np.maximum(b, tiny)
    return evaluate_scaled(b)


def edge_perturb_scaled(a, rng):
    b = a.copy()
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    e = max(2, n // 120)
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        b[:e] *= float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))
    else:
        b[-e:] *= float(rng.uniform(1.2, 3.0))
    b[0] *= float(rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8))
    b[-1] *= float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.5))
    b = np.maximum(b, np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    return evaluate_scaled(b)


def dense_gradient(w, a, n):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k c_k where c = a * a and c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}.
    Computed as 2 * Hankel product: grad_i = 2 * sum_j a_j w_{i+j}.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, L)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = w[:m]
        w = tmp
    maxw = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(maxw) or maxw <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = w / maxw
    rev = w[::-1].copy()
    N = ceil_power2(L + n - 1)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(rev, N)
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fa, N)
    if conv.size < 2 * n:
        tmp = np.zeros(2 * n, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:conv.size] = conv
        conv = tmp
    g = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return 2.0 * g


def local_search_maxfixed(a, c, n, deadline, rng, p0=4.0, max_step0=0.18, momentum=0.55):
    scaled = scale_to_max1(a, c)
    if scaled is None:
        return None
    a, c = scaled
    score = score_from_a_c(a, c)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return None

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    current_score = best_score
    last_improve = 0

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p = float(p0)
    max_step = float(max_step0)
    rejects = 0
    steps = 0
    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        elapsed = steps - last_improve

        # Adapt peak emphasis.
        if elapsed > 250:
            if p < 8.0:
                p = 8.0
                v.fill(0.0)
                max_step = 0.18
            elif p < 16.0:
                p = 16.0
                v.fill(0.0)
                max_step = 0.15
            elif p < 32.0:
                p = 32.0
                v.fill(0.0)
                max_step = 0.12
            elif p < 64.0:
                p = 64.0
                v.fill(0.0)
                max_step = 0.10
            else:
                p = float(p0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                max_step = 0.18

        # Diversify if the best has not moved.
        if elapsed > 1000 or rejects > 10:
            a = best_a.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            current_score = float(best_score)
            v.fill(0.0)
            rejects = 0

            res = random_perturb_scaled(a, rng, 0.20, bool(rng.random() < 0.5))
            if res is not None and (float(res[2]) < float(best_score) * 1.25 or rng.random() < 0.35):
                a = res[0].copy()
                c = res[1].copy()
                current_score = float(res[2])
                if current_score < best_score:
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                    best_score = current_score
                    last_improve = steps

        # Lp peak weights.
        w = np.power(c, p - 1.0)
        maxw = float(np.max(w)) if w.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(maxw) or maxw <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / maxw

        D = dense_gradient(w, a, n)
        if not np.isfinite(D).all():
            break

        # Tiny noise to soften exact symmetries.
        if rng.random() < 0.15:
            sd = float(np.std(D))
            if sd > 1e-15:
                D = D + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 * sd, n)

        v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * D
        vmin = float(np.min(v))
        vmax = float(np.max(v))
        span = vmax - vmin

        if span <= 1e-14:
            b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n))
        else:
            step = max_step * (v - vmin) / span
            b = a * np.exp(-step)

        b = np.maximum(b, tiny)
        cb = convolution_fft(b)
        sc = scale_to_max1(b, cb)
        if sc is None:
            max_step = max(0.02, max_step * 0.5)
            rejects += 1
            steps += 1
            continue

        b, cb = sc
        snew = score_from_a_c(b, cb)

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(snew):
            if snew <= current_score:
                accept = True
            else:
                tol = 0.01 + 0.05 / (1.0 + float(elapsed) / 500.0)
                if snew <= current_score * (1.0 + tol) and rng.random() < 0.25:
                    accept = True

        if accept:
            a = b
            c = cb
            current_score = float(snew)
            if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                best_score = current_score
                last_improve = steps
                print("local n={} best={:.6f} step={} p={:.1f}".format(n, best_score, steps, p))
            max_step = min(0.55, max_step * 1.04)
            rejects = 0
        else:
            max_step = max(0.02, max_step * 0.85)
            rejects += 1

        steps += 1
        if steps % 3000 == 0:
            print("local n={} best={:.6f} cur={:.6f} p={:.1f} step={:.3f}".format(
                n, best_score, current_score, p, max_step))

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def candidate_key(item):
    return float(item[2])


def search_length(n, rng, deadline, global_best):
    inits = []

    base = make_c_sequence(n)
    if base is not None:
        a, c = base
        s = score_from_a_c(a, c)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            inits.append((a.copy(), c.copy(), float(s)))

    if global_best is not None:
        gb = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if gb.size > 0:
            if gb.size > 16384:
                gb = stretch_sequence(gb, 16384)
            st = stretch_sequence(gb, n)
            res = evaluate_scaled(st)
            if res is not None and float(res[2]) < 1.70:
                inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))

    if inits:
        base_a = inits[0][0]
        for _ in range(3):
            res = random_perturb_scaled(base_a, rng, 0.25, bool(rng.random() < 0.5))
            if res is not None and float(res[2]) < 1.70:
                inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))
        for _ in range(3):
            res = edge_perturb_scaled(base_a, rng)
            if res is not None and float(res[2]) < 1.70:
                inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))

        for a0, c0, s0 in list(inits):
            b = 0.5 * (a0 + a0[::-1])
            res = evaluate_scaled(b)
            if res is not None and float(res[2]) < float(s0):
                inits.append((res[0].copy(), res[1].copy(), float(res[2])))

    if not inits:
        return None

    inits.sort(key=candidate_key)
    best_a, best_score = inits[0][0], float(inits[0][2])

    tries = min(2, len(inits))
    for idx in range(tries):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 30.0:
            break
        piece = rem / float(tries - idx)
        if piece <= 0.0:
            piece = max(10.0, rem / 2.0)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + piece)

        loc = local_search_maxfixed(inits[idx][0], inits[idx][1], n, sub_deadline, rng)
        if loc is not None and float(loc[1]) < best_score:
            best_a = loc[0]
            best_score = float(loc[1])

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Time-bounded hybrid search: fractional start, Lp multiplicative local search,
    random edge/low-frequency perturbations, and length scaling."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # If a previously found construction is available, try to use it.
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0:
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size > 16384:
                arr = stretch_sequence(arr, 16384)
            res = evaluate_scaled(arr)
            if res is not None:
                best_a = res[0].copy()
                best_score = float(res[2])
                print("height_sequence_1 score={:.6f} n={}".format(best_score, best_a.size))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # More lengths, increasingly important, but time-capped.
    length_plan = [
        (1024, 120.0),
        (2048, 500.0),
        (4096, 1200.0),
        (8192, 1200.0),
    ]

    for n, cap in length_plan:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 60.0:
            break
        slice_time = min(cap, rem - 40.0)
        if slice_time < 40.0:
            break
        sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + slice_time
        res = search_length(n, rng, sub_deadline, best_a)
        if res is not None:
            if float(res[1]) < best_score:
                best_a = res[0].copy()
                best_score = float(res[1])
                print("after n={} best={:.6f}".format(n, best_score))

    # Final polish if there is substantial time left.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_a is not None:
        ab = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if ab.size > 16384:
            ab = stretch_sequence(ab, 8192)
        c = convolution_fft(ab)
        loc = local_search_maxfixed(ab, c, ab.size, deadline, rng)
        if loc is not None and float(loc[1]) < best_score:
            best_a = loc[0].copy()
            best_score = float(loc[1])
            print("final polish best={:.6f}".format(best_score))

    # Fallback if everything failed.
    if best_a is None:
        base = make_c_sequence(1024)
        if base is None:
            a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
            best_a = a
        else:
            best_a = base[0].copy()

    # Return sum-normalized positive floats. Scale is invariant for the objective.
    fin = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if fin.size == 0:
        fin = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    fin = np.clip(fin, 0.0, 1000.0)
    fin = np.maximum(fin, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(fin))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        fin = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(fin))
    fin = fin / s

    out = []
    for x in fin:
        if float(x) > 1e-300:
            out.append(float(x))
        else:
            out.append(0.0)

    return out
```
